## 2026-08-24: Bridge mapped procurement orders to Derya supplier research

- Added an explicit `bourne` or `derya` mode flag, defaulting safely to
  `bourne`. In Derya mode, a protected cron reads only mapped customer orders
  awaiting research and queues at most two office-scoped Supply OS runs.
- The bridge uses a real active internal ADMIN from each mapped office and the
  existing workspace authorization path. It does not fabricate a session or
  write the workflow database.
- Added a protected shortlist endpoint for Bourne. It projects completed
  research into supplier candidates and deterministic RFQ drafts while
  preserving unknown commercial terms and public evidence provenance.
- RFQ output remains `DRAFT_ONLY` with `no_send: true`. No supplier contact,
  email, approval, quote, purchase, commitment, or workflow ledger write is
  performed by Derya.


## 2026-08-24: Harden procurement research bridge triggers

- Research inputs now use the supplier worker contract caps and are validated
  before enqueue. Incomplete orders are skipped with their missing fields.
- New submissions trigger a best-effort, non-blocking Derya enqueue in `derya`
  mode. The cron remains a backstop for active orders with no suppliers.
- Idempotency conflicts caused by changed notes are treated as reuse.
- Shortlist retrieval rejects malformed completed output as not found.
- Shadow operation is the first rollout: Bourne may continue its own discovery
  until it successfully consumes a Derya shortlist.


## 2026-08-25: Bulk RFQ review gate and reply visibility

- Bulk RFQ preview gained a draft-only mode so an operator can read every
  pending subject, recipient, and body before a campaign sends anything.
- Supplier contact lookup accepts nested JSON email locations, matching the
  procurement workflow fallback chain.
- A read-only replies endpoint reports outbound and inbound counts for one
  sourcing request and classifies inbound mail as reply, automatic reply,
  bounce, or wrong recipient, using the office-scoped local email ledger.

## 2026-08-25: Supplier conversations without a workflow receipt

- Reported symptom: a supplier that received an RFQ showed no conversation and
  the request read "4 of 169 contacted".
- Cause: the send is provider-confirmed and stored locally, but the workflow
  receipt write-back is rejected for the dashboard role, and both the contacted
  flag and the conversation reference were derived only from that receipt.
- Fix: derive contact and conversation from the office email ledger through the
  RFQ subject reference when the workflow receipt is absent.
- Verified with `npm run test:procurement-local-outreach`, plus the existing
  bulk RFQ contract test. No supplier message was sent for this change.

## 2026-08-25: RFQ outreach overlay reads the ledger once per list

- The local outreach overlay ran one subject scan per listed request, so the
  procurement list fired up to 50 concurrent unindexed scans.
- `localRfqOutreachByOrder` now runs a single scan for every listed order,
  without message bodies, and groups the rows by RFQ subject reference in
  memory. The single order helper reuses it.
